**Snapshot:** 2026-11-21, `origin/main` at `37feae1dc5efa6968f90d17a1eda6daed29afd4a`. Each of the 13 folders has exactly six tracked files: `README.md`, `BASES.json`, `SOURCES.json`, `candidate.py`, `fixtures.json`, and its sixth file above. Seven have `RESULT.md`; six use another report. Relative evidence anchors in a row begin at `workspace/candidates/<folder>/`.

**What landed:** all 13 packages landed as tracked records under `workspace/candidates/`. Twelve manifests record `installed: false` at `/installed`; CIW-08 says `CANDIDATE_ONLY / NOT_INSTALLED` at `/status`. None of the 13 `candidate.py` blob IDs has an exact copy at a tracked path outside `workspace/candidates/` on this snapshot. That rules out an unchanged copy, not a rewritten equivalent or a host installation. A fixture pass or a report on main does not prove a rollout. Reproduce the inventory and exact-copy comparison with `git ls-tree -r origin/main` (match the blob-ID column); inspect package history with `git log --oneline origin/main -- workspace/candidates/`.

**Baseline check:** `git diff --name-only 1f8510907cb2989bb50391fde45d6211e4735879 37feae1dc5efa6968f90d17a1eda6daed29afd4a` lists only three new offline checks (info-page rendering, TLD freshness, and item-standard gap comparison) and their tests; none is a path named in the candidate manifests, a candidate record, or this inventory. `git ls-tree -r <revision> -- reports/hass-npp/G-QUESTIONS.md` is empty on both revisions; `git log --all -- reports/hass-npp/G-QUESTIONS.md` and `git grep -l G-QUESTIONS origin/main -- docs reports workspace` found no tracked locator. No owner answers were inferred from that absence. The scope exclusions in the brief stand; this reads Git objects only. `AGENTS.md:3–5,13–17` sets the source and evidence boundaries.

## R2. Does each base still match?

Every recorded path-and-hash entry is in the folder tables below. **Same / Changed** mean the file exists and its *raw bytes* do / do not match the recorded SHA-256. The recorded file digests are 64-character content hashes, not the 40-character Git blob IDs: a direct comparison to `git rev-parse` is unequal by format, even in a **Same** row. The separate blob column gives the actual Git object ID. **External** means the manifest cites a source outside this checkout; no host path was opened or resolved. Package bindings are included but do not count as reused repo sources. The recorded inspection commits in R1 are provenance labels, not substitutes for the per-file digests (for example OLD-63 describes later pin refreshes at `RESULT.md:109–146`).

| Folder | Reused repo sources: same / changed / missing | External | Package bindings: same / changed / missing |

| `AUDINV-CIW-08` | 0 / 1 / 0 | 1 | 2 / 1 / 0 |

Reproduce from a checkout where `git rev-parse origin/main` is the snapshot above (otherwise substitute its full SHA for `origin/main`):

```sh
git ls-tree -r --name-only origin/main -- workspace/candidates/
git show origin/main:workspace/candidates/AUDINV-CIW-06/BASES.json
git rev-parse origin/main:.github/workflows/check.yml
git cat-file -t origin/main:.github/workflows/check.yml
git cat-file blob origin/main:.github/workflows/check.yml | sha256sum
```

Repeat the last three commands with the repo path in each row; resolve only package-local paths below their folder. The resolver used `git rev-parse origin/main:<path>` on each existing repo row after confirming membership in `git ls-tree -r origin/main`. Run `git show origin/main:<folder>/BASES.json` to read the recorded value at the pointer. For CIW-09 the note attached to the weights filename is not part of the path; for CIW-08 `/reused_sources/2` explicitly names its own receipt. External display labels omit absolute host prefixes; the exact locator remains in the cited manifest. No source was executed, and no historical receipt was refreshed.

## R3. Disagreements, missing files and overlap

- **README vs. RESULT:** no contradictory package verdict found in the seven pairs. Compare the R1 verdicts with `README.md:3` (OLD-57/58/59/62/63/69) and `README.md:27–31` (OLD-56). OLD-58’s internal `"status": "PASS"` (`RESULT.md:9–11`) is fixture evidence alongside `"installed": false`, not a contradiction or install. OLD-62 also records boundary-level HOLD/FAIL outcomes (`RESULT.md:62–89`), not a different package verdict. For OLD-57 and OLD-59 the first package verdict is the JSON `status` line; individual coverage outcomes appear earlier.
- **Missing:** no folders missing `README.md`; no folders missing `candidate.py`. The six marked **No** in R1 are missing `RESULT.md`, not their named report or reconciliation TSV. Reproduce with the tree listing above.
- **Overtaken in part, not adopted wholesale:** OLD-56 already found native generic-question context and assigned overlapping native integration elsewhere (`README.md:27–31`; `RESULT.md:10–19`). OLD-57 already reused photo ingress, orientation and install helpers (`RESULT.md:24–32`). OLD-58 says the historical first-record-only issue had already been addressed; its isolated decoded-image identity checks were extra (`RESULT.md:15–23`). OLD-63 reports that content-bundle operations already existed; the fixture proof did not close live backup/restore (`RESULT.md:5–14,148–154`). These are documented overlaps, not evidence that the packages replaced live tooling. No full supersession or installation is established for any folder by the checked evidence. A changed base means changed bytes, not a successor.
- **Package digest drift is separate:** the changed package rows in R2 are manifest-to-tree discrepancies, not README/RESULT verdict conflicts. Do not treat the associated historical execution as proof for these current package bytes.

## R4. Ranked next use

1. **Useful historical reports; preserve their limits:** CIW-09 (one-day suite cost), FIX-041 (exact-ID snapshot, not a decision), OLD-64 (source/enforcement distinction). Use the named R1 reports as dated evidence, not current measurements or approval.
2. **Fixture references only:** OLD-56/57/58/59/62/63/69. Recheck source overlap and changed bindings in R2/R3 before any separately admitted work. Nothing here qualifies real-model behavior, phone/provider behavior, site delivery, live restore, or owner acceptance (see the R1 results and each folder’s `SOURCES.json` missing-coverage fields).
3. **Leave measurement gaps explicit:** CIW-06/08/10. An absent day export, pass enumeration, or width contrast is not a basis to change a cap or timer (R1 report anchors).

**No buildable follow-up is needed for this inventory.** Existing files remain where they are. This audit neither resolves an evidence gap by running a candidate nor treats it as a reason to install one. No owner decision, live check, or configuration change was made.
